Begin Object Class=/Script/UnrealEd.MaterialGraphNode_Custom Name="MaterialGraphNode_Custom_0" ExportPath="/Script/UnrealEd.MaterialGraphNode_Custom'/Engine/Transient.M_Phase0_Test:MaterialGraph_0.MaterialGraphNode_Custom_0'"
   Begin Object Class=/Script/Engine.MaterialExpressionCustom Name="MaterialExpressionCustom_0" ExportPath="/Script/Engine.MaterialExpressionCustom'/Engine/Transient.M_Phase0_Test:MaterialGraph_0.MaterialGraphNode_Custom_0.MaterialExpressionCustom_0'"
   End Object
   Begin Object Name="MaterialExpressionCustom_0" ExportPath="/Script/Engine.MaterialExpressionCustom'/Engine/Transient.M_Phase0_Test:MaterialGraph_0.MaterialGraphNode_Custom_0.MaterialExpressionCustom_0'"
      Code="// === Gerstner ambient — 3 ondes empilées ===\r\n// D = direction propagation (sera normalisée), L = wavelength cm, A = amplitude cm,\r\n// S = speed cm/s, Q = steepness 0..1 (0 = sinusoïde pure, 1 = vague pointue type \"ocean\").\r\nconst float2 D1 = float2(1.0,  0.3); const float L1 = 90.0; const float A1 = 1.2; const float S1 = 30.0; const float Q1 = 0.05;\r\nconst float2 D2 = float2(-0.5, 1.0); const float L2 = 140.0; const float A2 = 0.6; const float S2 = 40.0; const float Q2 = 0.03;\r\nconst float2 D3 = float2(0.7, -0.5); const float L3 = 220.0;  const float A3 = 0.35; const float S3 = 5; const float Q3 = 0.02;\r\n\r\nfloat3 GerstnerOffset = float3(0, 0, 0);\r\n\r\n// Onde 1\r\nfloat k1 = 6.2831853 / L1;\r\nfloat omega1 = S1 * k1;\r\nfloat phase1 = k1 * dot(normalize(D1), WorldXY) + omega1 * Time;\r\nGerstnerOffset.x += Q1 * A1 * normalize(D1).x * cos(phase1);\r\nGerstnerOffset.y += Q1 * A1 * normalize(D1).y * cos(phase1);\r\nGerstnerOffset.z += A1 * sin(phase1);\r\n\r\n// Onde 2\r\nfloat k2 = 6.2831853 / L2;\r\nfloat omega2 = S2 * k2;\r\nfloat phase2 = k2 * dot(normalize(D2), WorldXY) + omega2 * Time;\r\nGerstnerOffset.x += Q2 * A2 * normalize(D2).x * cos(phase2);\r\nGerstnerOffset.y += Q2 * A2 * normalize(D2).y * cos(phase2);\r\nGerstnerOffset.z += A2 * sin(phase2);\r\n\r\n// Onde 3\r\nfloat k3 = 6.2831853 / L3;\r\nfloat omega3 = S3 * k3;\r\nfloat phase3 = k3 * dot(normalize(D3), WorldXY) + omega3 * Time;\r\nGerstnerOffset.x += Q3 * A3 * normalize(D3).x * cos(phase3);\r\nGerstnerOffset.y += Q3 * A3 * normalize(D3).y * cos(phase3);\r\nGerstnerOffset.z += A3 * sin(phase3);\r\n\r\nreturn GerstnerOffset;"
      Description="Geistner3Waves"
      Inputs(0)=(InputName="WorldXY",Input=(Expression="/Script/Engine.MaterialExpressionComponentMask'MaterialGraphNode_60.MaterialExpressionComponentMask_3'"))
      Inputs(1)=(InputName="Time",Input=(Expression="/Script/Engine.MaterialExpressionTime'MaterialGraphNode_59.MaterialExpressionTime_1'"))
      ShowCode=True
      MaterialExpressionEditorX=-1696
      MaterialExpressionEditorY=-1888
      MaterialExpressionGuid=A5ABDD394831C9501447F5879D8B8C33
      Material="/Script/UnrealEd.PreviewMaterial'/Engine/Transient.M_Phase0_Test'"
   End Object
   MaterialExpression="/Script/Engine.MaterialExpressionCustom'MaterialExpressionCustom_0'"
   NodePosX=-1696
   NodePosY=-1888
   NodeGuid=6FE4F0AA46664892E7C0519E511750F3
   CustomProperties Pin (PinId=91655C0F4E59452E23B3508D533C074C,PinName="WorldXY",PinType.PinCategory="required",PinType.PinSubCategory="",PinType.PinSubCategoryObject=None,PinType.PinSubCategoryMemberReference=(),PinType.PinValueType=(),PinType.ContainerType=None,PinType.bIsReference=False,PinType.bIsConst=False,PinType.bIsWeakPointer=False,PinType.bIsUObjectWrapper=False,PinType.bSerializeAsSinglePrecisionFloat=False,LinkedTo=(MaterialGraphNode_60 713C8D5C420011554BEE909914682BE8,),PersistentGuid=00000000000000000000000000000000,bHidden=False,bNotConnectable=False,bDefaultValueIsReadOnly=False,bDefaultValueIsIgnored=False,bAdvancedView=False,bOrphanedPin=False,)
